File Information
Collection: PokéAPI CI
Collection Description
Minimal API test suite for the public PokéAPI (https://pokeapi.co). Requests are grouped into v2.1 folders (pokemon, types, pagination, errors) and validate a known Pokémon, a Type resource and its documented damage relations, paginated lists, and 404s for nonexistent resources. The pagination folder asserts the shared list envelope once in a folder-level test script. The base URL is a collection variable so it can be changed in one place.
